Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any biodiversity module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 2. The projective resolution has length at most 756 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by biodiversity generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 2, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 756. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the biodiversity structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 180. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 658 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on biodiversity structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 180. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 658. QED.
